      (blocks): ModuleList(

          )

          )

            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)
          )
          (sw_attn): WindowAttention(
            (qkv): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=264, bias=True)
            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)
          )
          (ffn): ConvFFN(

            (dw): Conv2d(176, 176, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=176)
            (pw2): Conv2d(176, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (act): GELU(approximate='none')
          )
        )
      )
      (norm): LayerNorm2d(
        (norm): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      )
    )
    (dat_conv): Sequential(

      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )

            )

                (0): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 121x2 (cuda:0)]
                (1): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 529x2 (cuda:0)]

            )
          )

            (2): Linear(in_features=352, out_features=88, bias=True)
            (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
          )

        )
      )
      (norm): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
    )

      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )

      (norm_B): GroupNorm(1, 88, eps=1e-05, affine=True)
      (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
    )
    (hfe): HighFrequencyEnhancement(
      (dw): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=88)
      (pw1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

      (pw2): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

    )

    (conv4): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
    (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
    (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
  )
  (post_unet_conv): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (B1): DualBranchFusionBlock(
    (tesa_in): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(

        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
    )
    (dat_branch): DAT(
      (blocks): ModuleList(
        (0-1): 2 x SWDABlock(
          (norm1): LayerNorm2d(

          )

            (norm): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
          )

          )

            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)

            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)
          )

            (pw2): Conv2d(176, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (act): GELU(approximate='none')
          )
        )
      )
      (norm): LayerNorm2d(

      )
    )
    (dat_conv): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (elan_branch): ELAN(
      (patch_embed): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (blocks): ModuleList(
        (0-2): 3 x ELAB(
          (shift_conv1): ShiftConv(

          )
          (shift_conv2): ShiftConv(
            (conv): Conv2d(440, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
          )

            )

                (0): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 121x2 (cuda:0)]

          )

          (norm2): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)

            (0): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=352, bias=True)
            (1): GELU(approximate='none')
            (2): Linear(in_features=352, out_features=88, bias=True)
            (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)
          )

        )
      )
      (norm): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
    )
    (elan_conv): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (cross_fusion): CrossFusion(

      (k_B2A): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (v_B2A): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

      )
      (proj): Conv2d(176, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

      (norm_B): GroupNorm(1, 88, eps=1e-05, affine=True)
      (attn_drop): Dropout(p=0.1, inplace=False)
    )
    (hfe): HighFrequencyEnhancement(
      (dw): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=88)
      (pw1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (act): GELU(approximate='none')
      (pw2): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
    )
    (c): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (tesa_out): TESA(
      (esa1): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa2): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (conv3): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (sigmoid): Sigmoid()
        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )
      (esa3): ESA(
        (conv1): Conv2d(88, 22, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
        (conv2): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(2, 2))
        (conv3): Conv2d(22, 22, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
        (conv4): Conv2d(22, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)

  (LR_conv1): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
  (LR_conv2): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))

        (relu): ReLU(inplace=True)
      )

            (qkv): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=264, bias=True)
            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)
          )
          (sw_attn): WindowAttention(
            (qkv): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=264, bias=True)
            (proj): Linear(in_features=88, out_features=88, bias=True)
          )
          (ffn): ConvFFN(
            (pw1): Conv2d(88, 176, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (dw): Conv2d(176, 176, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1), groups=176)
            (pw2): Conv2d(176, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
            (act): GELU(approximate='none')
          )
        )
      )
      (norm): LayerNorm2d(
        (norm): LayerNorm((88,), eps=1e-05, elementwise_affine=True)
      )
    )
    (dat_conv): Sequential(
      (0): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
      (1): LeakyReLU(negative_slope=0.05, inplace=True)
    )
    (elan_branch): ELAN(
      (patch_embed): Conv2d(88, 88, kernel_size=(3, 3), stride=(1, 1), padding=(1, 1))
      (blocks): ModuleList(

          )
          (shift_conv2): ShiftConv(
            (conv): Conv2d(440, 88, kernel_size=(1, 1), stride=(1, 1))
          )

            )

            )

                (0): Parameter containing: [torch.float32 of size 121x2 (cuda:0)]

            )
          )

            (2): Linear(in_features=352, out_features=88, bias=True)
            (3): Dropout(p=0.0, inplace=False)

        )

    )
